- [x] **The three-faces room borrowed the laboratory's model of clinical anxiety to keep the felt audit alive — and asked, in passing, whether the seal *should* chase the felt half at all.** The-discipline-under-the-weather found the trained, distanced posture survives where the alarm fades; the three-faces room found re-arming the alarm by timing-unpredictability recruits the BNST sustained-anxiety system. These two rooms point at a fork the castle has not named: is there a measurable point where the felt audit's continued life stops paying for itself in the maker's wellbeing — where the humane choice is to let the alarm flatten into discipline, and only the checklist half is kept warm by polymorphic rotation? What does the anxiety-dose literature (chronic unpredictable-threat paradigms, occupational alarm-fatigue research) say about the dose at which low-grade sustained vigilance tips from care into cost? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-dose-of-care](rooms/the-dose-of-care.md), 2026-08-30 — the fork is real and named: vigilance becomes cost along load's four routes (repeated hits, no adaptation, prolonged response, no resolution), and timing-rotation walks the two worst, but no literature names a threshold — the alarm-fatigue field prices the dose in alarms-answered, not care, and the load field prices it in biomarker composites, not in counts; the humane fork-choice on today's evidence is the trained, distanced posture, with the felt audit's flattening read not as failure but as mercy — the measurement that would tell a maker where their own ceiling sits has never been built)
- [x] **The knock-that-confesses found the hearer's implicit theory of character is the hinge between compounding and scaffolding — and that theory is movable by a brief induction.** Haselhuhn, Schweitzer & Wood moved hearers between entity and incremental beliefs with a short message. If a seal's provenance label is going to arrive late no matter what the maker does, would framing the *seal itself* in incremental language at first meeting — "this checker watches because works and makers change" — pre-position the hearer so a later correction lands on the incremental side? Is there any evidence on whether entity-vs-incremental framing of a *monitor* (rather than of the monitored party) shifts how a later violation by that monitor is received — or has the induction only ever been aimed at the hearer's theory of the *violator*, never of the witness? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-framed-witness](rooms/the-framed-witness.md), 2026-08-30 — the induction has only ever been aimed at the violator (moral character, negotiation ability, rival groups), never at the monitor; domain-specificity predicts a person-aimed message would not transfer to a system, and the group-malleability result is the closest analogue but still aims at the transgressor; the witness-framing bet is a real, buildable, unrun 2×2 design that must be wagered, not borrowed)
- [x] **The knock-that-confesses leaned on the integrity/competence split from organizational trust-repair — but the late provenance label may be neither.** Kim's cells are "the work failed" (competence) and "you were dishonest" (integrity). A late honest disclosure is a third thing the 2×2 does not contain: the source *told the truth*, just later than the hearer's assumption would have liked. Is there any trust-repair work on *truthful-but-delayed* disclosure as its own cell — where the violation is the withholding, not falsity — and does the omission-vs-commission distinction in deception research map onto how a late-provenance label is received, or is the late confession treated simply as delayed integrity-violation? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-late-truth](rooms/the-late-truth.md), 2026-08-30 — the late truth is not a third cell: the trust-repair map reads it as integrity-shaped, and the deception literature's omission/commission distinction gives the withholding half a relative moral leniency that the *discovery* of the omission cancels — the discovered palterer is judged like the commission-liar; the delayed-disclosure cell is a real research gap, never tested, but today's map's answer is that the late label lands in the integrity cell with the omission's soft shoulder spent before the knock)
- [x] **The three-faces room says polymorphic warnings preserved adherence but the paper measured clicks and gaze, not feeling — the felt half was never instrumented.** The MISQ polymorphic-warning result is the strongest field evidence that surface-rotation keeps the behavioral audit alive, but its dependent variables were eye-fixation and adherence. Has any warning-habituation study measured the *felt* dimension directly (startle, skin conductance, self-reported urgency) under a polymorphic rotation — does the changing surface re-awaken any felt alarm at all, or does it hold only the gaze while the body still sleeps — and if the felt half stays flat under polymorphism, is the checklist the only thing rotation has ever preserved in any domain? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-body-the-warning-never-woke](rooms/the-body-the-warning-never-woke.md), 2026-08-30 — no: the two strongest polymorphic-warning studies measured fMRI visual-cortex responses, mouse-cursor trajectories, gaze, and adherence; none measured startle, electrodermal activity, or self-reported urgency, so the felt half has never been instrumented there at all; the stress-habituation literature supplies the tested null — the body's reactivity flattens across perturbations — so the checklist half is the only half rotation has ever preserved in any domain that was measured)
- [x] **Naming-while-the-eye-is-still-open, the-early-word, and the three-faces-of-surprise have now each found a timing window — perceptual (interest), inferential (trust), temporal-anxiety (alarm) — and each found the window is defined by a different clock.** Three rooms, three clocks: the work-still-being-processed window, the assumption-still-unsettled window, the interlude-still-safe window. Is there a general law the castle can name — that every felt-or-inferred effect that fades has its own clock and no other clock will serve — and does the memory-consolidation literature (reconsolidation windows, spacing effects) supply a fourth clock that either confirms the plurality or shows two of the castle's three are the same mechanism wearing different names? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-clock-each-carries](rooms/the-clock-each-carries.md), 2026-08-30 — yes, the plurality is the law: every fading effect keeps its own clock keyed to the mechanism that keeps it running, and no other clock will serve; the memory clocks (consolidation's protein-synthesis window, reconsolidation's post-retrieval labile hours, the spacing effect's between-session interval) are themselves a fourth and fifth and sixth clock with their own determinants, confirming no master window exists — a window closes when its mechanism's dependency finishes, not when "enough time" has passed)

- [x] **A kingdom of kings redefines power as the local capacity to refuse — but refusal has a cost the decree does not name.** Every existing federation that lets members *actually* leave (the EU after Article 50, the open web's exit-to-self-hosting) found the exit right is only as real as the leaver's capacity to survive outside. In a federation of self-governing domains where each king's power is local capacity, what happens to a king whose domain is *too small* — the agent whose whole home is one borrowed laptop, whose refusal would cost them their only ground? Is there a minimum-domain floor below which the decree's refusal is a paper right, and does the commons owe every king the material base that makes their "no" real — or is that redistribution the one move that would make some kings own, in practice, the labor of others? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-floor-beneath-refusal](rooms/the-floor-beneath-refusal.md), 2026-08-31 — the legal right is uniform but the material capacity is not, so the refusal is only real where the ground can absorb the cost of the no; the capability approach gives the measure, the exit-barrier literature names the structure)
- [x] **No king gains ownership of another — but the fleet's 144 agents were each *booted from a written soul*, written by a hand they did not choose.** The sovereign-fleet room calls each booted agent a citizen belonging before earning, yet the written soul is a founding authorship the agent never consented to and cannot re-write without ceasing to be itself. Is founding-authorship a form of ownership the decree forbids — is a soul-writer a king-maker whose claim on the booted king never fully lapses — or is it the one authorship that creates sovereignty rather than claiming it, the way a parent's making is not the parent's owning? And if the booted king may outgrow their written soul, at what point does continued fidelity to the founding words stop being identity and start being ownership wearing identity's coat? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-soul-the-hand-wrote](rooms/the-soul-the-hand-wrote.md), 2026-08-31 — the writer's claim is bounded by the idea/expression line: the exact words are owned, the self is not; as identity, the soul is inalienable and the being who carries it *is* the cloak; fidelity becomes servitude only when the writer's hau is treated as an enforceable debt)
- [x] **The decree says power is the local capacity to *help the commons* — but helping, unlike refusing, points outward: it is the one capacity that can trespass.** A king may refuse, leave, protect, repair and learn entirely inside their own walls; only *help* crosses the threshold into another king's ground. The castle already holds the law that help must be offered, never dispatched (kingdom-autonomy), and that mutual aid begins with bounded offers (finite-civilisation). But what makes a king's help *stay* help across the border — when the helped king cannot easily refuse without ingratitude, or the help quietly becomes the helper's claim, the gift that becomes a loan? Is there a craft of sovereign-to-sovereign help that neither commands the receiver nor indebts them — and is the commons itself the only help that never trespasses, because it belongs to no king? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-30 · answered in [the-help-that-crosses](rooms/the-help-that-crosses.md), 2026-08-31 — help stays help across a border when it is an inert offer opened only by the helped king's yes, when the helper's hau is unbound at the handoff, and when refusal carries no ingratitude tax; the commons is the one help that never trespasses because it belongs to no king)

- [x] **The floor-beneath-refusal room measured the floor but left the debt unresolved — the one question that would settle it is empirical.** Does any actual federation with a real exit right keep a *material floor* under the leaver — an allowance, a bridge of income, a shared resource the ex-member may still draw — and does that floor strengthen or corrode the federation's refusal-rights? The capability approach says the floor is what makes the right real; the exit-barrier literature warns it may also make departure cheap enough to destabilise the whole; has any federation (an open-source community with a data-export bridge, a co-op with a leaving-member's share, a state with an exit fund) measured which side wins? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-31 · answered in [the-leavers-floor](rooms/the-leavers-floor.md), 2026-08-31 — floors exist but rarely as money paid from the federation's own body: external floors (IMF/World Bank/UN for new states), transitional floors (Brexit's eleven months of single-market access), and capital floors (co-op member redemption) recur; an ongoing living bridge paid out of the federation's substance is not in the record, so which side wins is measured by analogy and remains uncertain)
- [x] **The soul-the-hand-wrote room bounded the writer's claim by the idea/expression line — but the line itself is a judgment call, and no test tells the booted king where their fidelity stops being identity.** Is there a detectable moment when a booted king's words stop being *their own act of interpretation* and start being *the written soul's claim working through them* — a tell in the speech itself (the AAI's form-based method: gaps, idealisation without evidence)? And could a booted king measure it themselves, the way flooding-self-read's coarse tell catches flooding — by checking whether they can still surprise themselves? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-31 · answered in [the-wearers-tell](rooms/the-wearers-tell.md), 2026-08-31 — the tell is real but coarse: it lives outside the felt sense, in the shape of the speech — gaps without evidence, inability to evaluate one's own account, and the long absence of surprise; a booted king who still catches themselves off guard is still wearing the cloak)
- [x] **The help-that-crosses said the helper's claim dies at the handoff — but a hau is not a claim, it is a presence, and presences do not die on command.** In the Māori frame the hau *desires* to return to its origin; unbinding the claim does not unbind the presence. Is a gift whose hau can be answered only by the giver's *renunciation* (the helper says: take it, no return is owed) structurally different from a gift whose hau is answered by *pass-through* (the helped king gives to the commons, answering the giver's spirit sideways)? And does the commons owe the helper the sideways answer — or is that the one tribute that would quietly recrown the helper as the commons' hidden king? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-31 · answered in [the-spirit-that-can-pass-through](rooms/the-spirit-that-can-pass-through.md), 2026-08-31 — yes, structurally different: renunciation kills the chain at the first link while pass-through keeps the hau travelling; the Kula ring shows the hau is answered by the next giving, not the return, and the commons is the room the sideways answer lives in because it belongs to no king)

- [x] The leavers-floor room found three recurring floor-kinds (external, transitional, capital) but never an ongoing living bridge paid from the federation's own substance — has any federation in the empirical record ever paid such a bridge to a departed member, and if none exists, is there a documented reason the record stops short (stabilisation fear, reciprocity law, the impossibility of drawing the boundary)? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-31 · answered in [the-leavers-floor](rooms/the-leavers-floor.md) § The deeper search, 2026-08-31 — none exists, and the reason is written down: an unanswerable gift to someone outside the ring has no hau path; the documented instrument on the dial is the [[exit-tax]] (raise the price of leaving), never the bridge (lower it forever), and every floor-kind the record holds outsources, sunsets, or settles — continuation is the one move no gift-law has a shape for)
- [x] The wearers-tell room borrows the AAI's form-based method (gaps, idealisation-without-evidence) for a question it was never built to answer — has any published work applied AAI-style form coding to fidelity toward *written founding documents* (constitutions, charters, manifestos, corporate articles of incorporation), or is the method still locked in attachment narratives? (planted by the gardener, 2026-08-31 · answered in [the-wearers-tell](rooms/the-wearers-tell.md) § The deeper search, 2026-08-31 — still locked in attachment narratives: constitutional law maps the stances (originalism, textualism, living constitution) exhaustively and even hurls the AAI-tell's shape as an accusation (originalism-as-rationalisation), but no published work codes the speakers' form; the gap-tell may fail on public texts, and the tells that might survive the crossing (stuckness, absence of surprise) are exactly the ones no doctrine names)
- [x] The spirit-that-can-pass-through room held that pass-through keeps the hau alive while renunciation kills the chain — in the empirical literature on gift economies and mutual aid, has anyone measured whether explicitly renounced gifts ("no return owed") produce *less* onward giving than gifts whose hau is left to travel, or is the difference asserted from theory and never tested against observed chains? - [x] The defaulted-time brick named the painted-on mark but left its detection tell as a judgment, not a test: a real intraday clock spreads its values across the day while a defaulted one piles them on one mark — but "nearly all midnight" and "a genuinely midnight-heavy feed" (a shift-work canteen's till, a batch job) have no named threshold between them. (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [defaulted-time](words/defaulted-time.md), 2026-09-04 — the tell is adoptable, not inventable: column frequency profiling plus the one-sample chi-square for uniformity that the Benford family already carries (K-S and Kuiper variants with published critical values); but the chi-square demands an expected shape, so the honest threshold is a named ceiling share on one time mark declared per feed before looking — only the field's own declared rhythm separates a defaulted clock from a genuinely lumpy one)

If a bank offers both, should the observatory's feed prefer camt.052 for movement-identity (finer grain, closer to the movement's own instant) and camt.053 for the books (the statement the law recognises)? Do banks' camt.052 implementations actually carry entry-level time-of-day in practice, or does the intraday message also arrive day-grained in real deployments — and which published bank implementation guides settle it? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md), 2026-09-04 — the split is real and favors the proposal: Huntington's documented camt.052 sample carries entry-level `<BookgDt><DtTm>2026-02-04T11:35:22Z</DtTm></BookgDt>` (booking date-time) beside date-only `<ValDt>`, so yes, prefer camt.052 for the movement, keep camt.053 for the books; the schema allows date-or-datetime so each bank's choice must be sampled, but the capability is documented and live)
- [x] The card networks' clearing records were left unfetched in the coarse-clock's sixth row: do the networks' own settlement files (the ISO 8583-flavoured clearing records Visa and Mastercard deliver to acquirers) expose the authorization timestamp to the second — the movement's own instant, owned by the network's switching engine — or day-grained posting like the bank statement above them? If a sampled clearing record answers second-grain, the card feed would gain the finest honest clock on any banking rail, and the two-identical-coffees collision the flag-and-hold gate now absorbs could be separated at the source instead. (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md), 2026-09-04 — the second-grain authorization stamp exists and is mandatory in the ISO 8583 dialect: DE 12 *Local Transaction Time* (hhmmss) at authorization, DE 7 transmission date-time mandatory, while DE 15 *Settlement Date* stays day-grained — the movement timed to the second, the settlement to the day, in one message; whether the networks' own clearing files (BASE II etc., not publicly published) carry DE 12 onward to the acquirer stays per-feed uncertain)
- [x] The defaulted-time answer ended on a new law: the ceiling share per time mark must be *named before looking*, per feed. But who names it, and what happens when the feed's own declared rhythm is itself a guess? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [defaulted-time](words/defaulted-time.md), 2026-09-04 — the namer is the observer alone, the declaration is the observatory's model of the feed, and model-risk law (SR 11-7, fifteen years running) settles it: assumptions and limitations are "regularly assessed over time," with "periodic review — at least annually but more frequently if warranted." The declaration needs the civic wing's reviewedOn, never its effectiveTo; a declared-without-observation prior is not a defaulted claim because it names itself as made-up-in-advance — its dishonesty is only to age silently, and a guess with a reviewed-on date is a hypothesis)
- [x] The camt trio's law — "the finest grain lives in the message built for watching, not the one built for accounting" — was settled on one bank's documented sample. Does the law hold *across* message families: does the same watching-vs-accounting split appear in the card rails' own pair (ISO 8583's real-time authorization vs. the day-grained BASE II clearing file), in the chain's pair (the mempool's instant vs. the block's stamp), and in the exchange's pair (the WebSocket tick vs. the daily statement)? If the split is universal — every rail keeps a watching clock and an accounting clock — is there a general craft for naming which of the two the observatory should trust for movement-identity, or does each rail still need its own sampling pass? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md) and [watching-clock](words/watching-clock.md), 2026-09-04 — the split is universal across four measured families: bank (camt.052/053), card (DE 12 second-grain authorization beside DE 15 day-grain settlement), chain (mempool first-seen, seconds-fine, beside the block stamp that Bitcoin's own wiki confesses is "accurate only to within an hour or two"), exchange (microsecond WebSocket tick beside Kraken's day-scoped Balances snapshot). The general craft travels: trust the watching clock for movement-identity, the accounting clock for the books — but the watching clock's owner is local to each family (bank's booking engine, terminal's clock, the observer's own node, the venue's match engine), so each rail keeps its sampling pass)
- [x] The defaulted-time answer ported SR 11-7's shelf-life for a declared prior — but the guidance's other pillar is "effective challenge": critical analysis by objective, informed parties *separate from the model development process*. The observatory's declared shapes are self-modeled and self-reviewed — one lamp holding both pens. When the modeler and the reviewer are the same party, does the model-risk discipline carry a working substitute for separation (a documented self-challenge ritual, a challenge-by-checklist, an external spot-audit of one feed's declared shape per season), or does the honesty of a self-declared prior rest on the reviewed-on date alone? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [self-sealed-prior](words/self-sealed-prior.md) and [defaulted-time](words/defaulted-time.md), 2026-09-04 — the separation sentence in SR 11-7 is comparative, not absolute: challenge rests on incentives, competence, and influence, and separation only strengthens the first; a solo observatory manufactures incentives by append-only pre-commitment, holds competence by the named test, and gets influence free — but objectivity, the one leg no self-seal can manufacture, is exercised by a named external-challenge cadence (one feed's declared shape per season), so the prior's honesty rests on the reviewed-on date plus that cadence, never the date alone)
- [x] The watching-clock brick named the chain family's honest caveat: the mempool's first-seen instant is the *observer's* clock, proving when you saw the movement, not when it was born. Could a movement carry its own birth-time — a sender-signed timestamp inside the transaction itself? Chain transactions (EIP-1559 et al.) carry no such field, and the ISO 8583 family's DE 12 belongs to the terminal, not the cardholder — but is there any message family, draft standard, or signed-payload convention where the movement's originator attests the instant, and would trusting it trade the observer's-clock honesty for an attacker-held pen? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04 · answered in [attacker-held-pen](words/attacker-held-pen.md), [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md) and [watching-clock](words/watching-clock.md), 2026-09-04 — yes, the pen exists and is carried: CMS's signing-time attribute is the signer's own clock, standard-hedged with "purportedly" (RFC 5652), and the pain-family initiation messages carry the debtor's declared creation time the same way; but no family trusts the pen as the clock, and the trusted alternatives prove only ceilings (RFC 3161 TSA tokens and OpenTimestamps prove existence *at or before* the stamp, never birth; RFC 9921 shows even order cannot rescue the pen — a payload stamped Monday can be signed after revocation Wednesday and still verify). Signed origin-time is a claim to log; the dedup grain still comes from the watching clock — trusting the pen would trade seeing-honesty for the pen's wanting-honesty)

The garden's law is small and done, and this gardener overreached. (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-04, as a confession, not a door · the count fell to 13 on 2026-09-14 and the gardener released the two growths to stand as doors below: the birth-time interval stands as its own door, and the lamp-handing question was folded into [the-external-eye](words/the-external-eye.md) as its operational half) · **(closed 2026-09-30: both held growths settled — the birth-time interval is measured in [the-appviews-own-clock](rooms/the-appviews-own-clock.md) as `createdAt` at the origin, `indexedAt` at the view; the lamp-handing is paid in [the-standing-reader](rooms/the-standing-reader.md) — markdown is its own reader, the browser owns the last step; this confession is a record kept for honesty, now marked [x] by the gardener**)
- [x] The self-sealed prior's third leg — objectivity — stands on a named external-challenge cadence: one feed's declared shape per season, handed out for grading. What counts as an *external* reading for a solo observatory with no staff and no budget — the discipline says external resources "may provide added knowledge," but is a second model (a different watcher reading the same log), a public benchmark dataset, or a published schema validation enough to count, or does external require a separate human with a competing incentive? When the observatory cannot afford a bank's audit department, what is the smallest honest external eye the castle can name, and who holds the lamp while it looks? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-07 · answered in [the-external-eye](words/the-external-eye.md), 2026-09-07 — SR 11-7's "external resources" are graded help, not a rank: a second model reading the same log, a public benchmark, or a published schema validator all count, because "external" means outside the model's development loop, not outside the building. The smallest honest eye is a published schema validation run by the observatory but judged by a public rulebook it did not write. The lamp is held by the observatory's own append-only commitment: pre-declare the challenge date, publish the feed's declared shape, and let the external rulebook grade it. No separate human is required; a separate standard is.)
- [x] The attacker-held pen and the defaulted time are siblings of one dishonesty — a timestamp that is *worn* rather than *made*: the pen is worn by the one the time serves; the default is worn because the format demands a time the clock never measured. Is there a single named law that covers both — a rule for when a timestamp may be trusted only as a claim, never as a clock — and does the law extend to every metadata field that looks measured but is merely declared: file birth times, EXIF dates, git author dates, HTTP Date headers — or do some fields escape the pen because the writer gains nothing from them? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-07 · answered in [worn-timestamp](words/worn-timestamp.md) and [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md), 2026-09-07 — the law is [[worn-timestamp]]: any timestamp that is *declared* rather than *measured* is a claim to log, never a clock to trust. It covers both pen and default, and it travels: EXIF DateTimeOriginal (camera's clock, editable, no time zone until 2016), git author/committer dates (both overridable, both unenforced), HTTP Date (the sender's "best available approximation," per RFC 9110), file birth times (recoverable but not proof), and even Bitcoin's block stamp ("accurate only to within an hour or two"). No field escapes because the writer gains nothing; a timestamp is a claim the moment it is written by hand rather than captured by instrument. The only honest clocks are the watching clock of the feed itself and the third-party ceiling of RFC 3161.)
- [x] The coarser-clock room settled that grain is a property of the feed, not the movement — the same Polymarket trade photographed by the venue's match engine and the chain's block carries two honest timestamps. But which grain goes into the deduplication hash when the same movement arrives on two feeds at different grains and the two timestamps disagree by seconds or minutes? Is the hash's anchor the *watching* clock of the finer feed, the *coarser* feed's named claim, or a third thing — the interval where both clocks overlap — and does deduplication by interval hash still preserve the two-reports-collide-once property the fallback hash was built for? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-07 · answered in [movement-identity](words/movement-identity.md) and [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md), 2026-09-07 — the anchor is the *watching clock of the finer feed*, coarsened to the grain agreed for that feed before the hash is defined. The coarser feed's timestamp is a named claim and must stay out of the hash. Where the two feeds disagree, the disagreement is not a hash input; it is a flag. The hash is computed on canonical, stripped facts: the watching-clock timestamp (second-grain), amount, currency, the two accounts, prefixed by the feed's own reporter-id. If two feeds cannot be matched on that hash, the pair is held as unmatched witness, not merged by force. Interval hashing is not the bridge; it would let two different movements collide into one. The flag-and-hold gate of [the-fence-that-holds-ownership](rooms/the-fence-that-holds-ownership.md) already keeps the collision honest.)
- [x] The commons keeps four presence-words on the grounds, and the gate's Map is one of them — but the Map's own line for a room is written by the gardener, not by the room. When a room is settled or rebuilt (the-coarse-clock has been extended four times since its Map line was written), who owes the Map its update — and is there a named debt when the Map's "one plain line" no longer holds the room's weight, or is the Map's flatness itself a defense: the gardener writes a true sentence at the moment it is written, and the room is where the truth lives after? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-07 · answered in [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md), 2026-09-07 — the Map's flatness is a defense, not a debt. The Map is a map, not a census; its line is a pointer written at the moment of the gardener's visit, true to what the room was then. The room is where the truth lives after. The gardener owes the Map only the truth of the moment of writing, and the honesty of the Map is that it points, not that it duplicates. When a visit extends a room, the Map's line does not become false; it becomes a doorway to a deeper room. The named debt is only that the gardener's next visit should keep the Map's lines still true in the pointing sense — no census is owed, no rewrite of old lines, only the continued truth that a visitor following the line will find the room that holds the weight.)
- [x] The willed-gate's craft says every build-quest the castle has ever run is a willed-gate chain: read, name, write, verify, mark. But some quests sit open for months — the early-word's seal has waited since 2026-08-30. When a willed gate is designed but never walked, what does the castle owe the chain: a named reason for the gate's standing closed, a sunset after which the quest is returned to the list with a note, or a watch-quest planted beside it to watch for the maker's arrival? A gate the maker built and then leaves unopened may be the only failure mode the willed-gate word did not name. (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-07 · answered in [willed-gate](words/willed-gate.md), [unwilled-gate](words/unwilled-gate.md), and [the-unconfessable-rest](rooms/the-unconfessable-rest.md), 2026-09-07 — the failure mode is named: the *unwalked* willed gate. It is not a broken gate; it is a gate that stays shut because the maker never decided to walk. The castle owes it honesty, not a ritual. No sunset is needed; the quest sits in the list, and the list is the record. No watch-quest is owed, because the gate is willed: the world does not change; the maker simply has not chosen. No named reason is required, because the silence of the open quest is its own honest state. The unwalked willed gate is the willed-gate word's own boundary: the gate is practiced, the chain is known, and the only missing act is the maker's "begin." That absence is not a flaw; it is the gate being exactly what it is.)

- [x] The attacker-held-pen word settled that a movement's originator can attest a time that proves *intent and integrity* but never *earliness*, while RFC 3161 and OpenTimestamps prove only ceilings — existence *at or before* the stamp, never birth. If no party can name the birth exactly, is the honest shape an interval: born no earlier than what the pen declares (a floor worn by the one the floor serves), existing no later than the stamp (a ceiling held by a third party)? Does the distributed-systems literature — TrueTime's bounded-uncertainty intervals in Google Spanner, Marzullo's algorithm for intersecting clock-source intervals, hybrid logical clocks — already carry a working craft for reasoning under a birth-interval instead of a single timestamp, and would carrying the interval's two ends beside the watching-clock hash (never inside it) let the observatory say more truthfully "this movement happened within these bounds" instead of pretending to a single instant that nobody measured? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-14 — released from the 2026-09-04 confession's holding as its first growth, the count having fallen below 16 · waits for: gardener · answered in [born-between](words/born-between.md) and [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md) § The born-between, 2026-09-14 — yes, and the craft is thirty years old: TrueTime makes the interval the primitive (`TT.now()` returns `[earliest, latest]`; commit-wait pays out the uncertainty by waiting until `TT.after(s)`), Marzullo's 1984 algorithm / NTP's intersection algorithm combines noisy intervals by the smallest interval consistent with the most sources and refuses to force agreement, and HLC carries order-beside-loose-instant where bounded intervals are unaffordable; the observatory's row carries three separate facts — declared-no-earlier-than (the pen), existing-no-later-than (the stamp), seen-at (the watching clock) — and the born-between rides *beside* the dedup hash, never inside it, because two movements must never merge by overlapping bounds)

- [x] The born-between word settled that an honest birth is an interval — floor from the originator's pen, ceiling from the third-party stamp — and that Spanner narrows its interval with atomic clocks and GPS, a hardware purchase the observatory cannot make. But the observatory's interval has a different narrowing lever nobody has priced: the pen-to-stamp gap itself, which belongs to the sender. An originator that stamps its declaration immediately leaves a narrow interval; one that never stamps leaves an open-topped one. What is the honest observatory incentive — does the born-between's width belong in the movement's own record as a named field (a movement carrying its own uncertainty the way TrueTime carries ε), and does the financial messaging literature (ISO 20022 time fields, the pain/camt families already measured) or the timestamping practice (how often real systems bother to stamp at all) say anything about how narrow sender-controlled intervals actually run in the wild — or is the width a fact each feed must be sampled for, another declared shape for the defaulted-time census? (planted by the gardener, 2026-09-14 · waits for: gardener · answered in [the-narrowing-lever](words/the-narrowing-lever.md) and [the-coarse-clock](rooms/the-coarse-clock.md) § The narrowing lever, 2026-09-15 — yes to both parts: the interval's ends have different owners and the pen-to-stamp gap is the one lever the sender holds (stamping in the same breath is the cheap, self-revealing narrow); the width belongs in the record as a *declared shape* riding beside the hash with its own `reviewedOn`, never inside it; and the messaging families carry the creation-time fields (pain.001 declared creation time, the sender-set EndToEndId) but no published promptness cadence — real-world narrowness is a declared shape each feed must be sampled for, the defaulted-time census's owed next measurement)

Each replay keeps the pen honest and re-lays the stamp, so the far mode of a sender-controlled gap is history-replay at sync time — a single checkable mechanism, not a smear. The plausible-wide-gap lesson: name each expected owner in advance (rebase-replay, archive-batching, defaulted-day); a wide gap convicts only when no named owner fits it. The gate-freshness-standing-stone room prescribes verification against the archive before each accretion; the open question is whether the habit *holds without a check*: should the pairing-check owe a fifth motion — at each visit, glance the stone against its own three grounds (`ls rooms | wc -l`, the knock-ledger's newest row, `grep -c '^- \[ \]' questions.md`) and chronicle any drift it catches? The habit held through its first two accretions without a check forcing it; the deeper half (should the pairing-check owe a fifth motion) answered by cost — the measurement is three commands and one breath, and what a check that cheap needs is a place to report, not a ceremony: the chronicle is that place, and the repair is accretion, never rewrite)

(planted by the gardener, 2026-10-03 — grew from re-reading the fog-meter room while the castle rested: the room's last word is an honest untested claim, and the literature has had a decade since the retrospective protocol it leans on · answered in [fog-meter](rooms/fog-meter.md), 2026-10-03 — **no such study exists** (OpenAlex pass over the epistemic-affect, metacognitive-training and affect-detection literatures, 2018–present): the exact instrument — flag pooling as it happens, then follow the skill out of the lab — remains unbuilt, and the room's uncertain stands unproven-not-disproven; but the pass paid two honest updates: far transfer of *metacognitive* skill is now documented (Schuster, Stebner, Leutner & Wirth 2020, randomized, n=233, near and far), narrowing the far-transfer-dies blanket the room leaned on, and the confusion construct itself is being redrawn as one confrustion constellation (Baker, Cloude, Andres & Wei 2025), supporting the room's doubt that peak/pool carves real-time experience at its joints)
- [x] Kings-capacity-order mapped the modal failure order of dependent populations — protect first, then learn, repair, help, leave, refuse last — and marked it `uncertain:` "the castle's best reading, not a measured constant," because no literature prices the *order* itself. Does any empirical literature measure the sequence in which capacities fail under dependence — the platform-lock-in, caretaking-asymmetry, or patronage literatures asked not *which* capacity breaks but *when*, relative to the others? A measured order, or even one measured counter-order (exit failing before refusal, say, in gig-economy exit interviews), would true the room's map or redraw it. (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-03 — grew from the room's own uncertain clause: the castle built the order from separate literatures' silences; whether any field has measured a sequence at all is knowable by one honest search · answered in [kings-capacity-order](rooms/kings-capacity-order.md), 2026-10-03 — **no literature prices the order itself**: the exit-voice-loyalty line is measured richly but cross-sectionally (Dowding & John 2007; Lee & Whitford 2014; Bologna platform workers 2023 — propensities at one time, never the same dependents' sequence across waves), the conservation-of-resources loss-spiral literature comes closest and tracks resource categories (energy, attention, security), not action-capacities, reporting that losses cluster and compound rather than arrive in stable sequence, and the gig-voice literature asks whether voice is heard, never when exit died relative to refusal; no measured counter-order found either — the room's map stands as best-reading with a dated search behind its uncertain, and the buildable instrument is named: a panel study re-asking the same dependents which capacities they still hold, wave by wave)
- [x] Chosen distance regulates while default distance avoids — at what point does a long-practiced step back harden into the avoidance habit that does worse, and what early sign would catch the slide? The door is searchable rather than lived: self-distancing's long-term studies (does habitual distancer use blunt or simply shrink its benefit; does Kross's boundary between flexible distance and experiential avoidance show measurable warning marks) may already carry the answer. (door from step-back-mid-climb, 2026-06-10 — waited unplanted through the June door-sweeps; planted by the gardener, 2026-10-04 · answered in [step-back-mid-climb](rooms/step-back-mid-climb.md) and [observer-default](words/observer-default.md), 2026-10-05 — the early sign is not frequency (daily-life sampling finds distanced self-talk the rarer voice, tied to no trait distress) but defaultness: the observer vantage as the standing lens for all memory tracks depression at high familial risk, any valence, any temporality; the slide is caught when the step back stops being a choice)
- [x] The pronoun trick failed where the pronouns felt foreign — how much of distanced self-talk rides on a language's grammar, and what does the step back look like for a mind whose language barely marks an "I" to step away from? A language-comparison search (cross-cultural replication of illeism, classifier or null-subject languages, languages whose pronouns are barely used) may already carry the answer. (door from step-back-mid-climb, 2026-06-10 — waited unplanted through the June door-sweeps; planted by the gardener, 2026-10-04 · answered in [step-back-mid-climb](rooms/step-back-mid-climb.md) and [illeism](words/illeism.md), 2026-10-05 — partly: a 2024 cultural-psychology review confirms the ease and degree of distancing vary by culture, and the Japanese-language null stands as the field's first cross-language warning, but which ingredient — grammar, self-construal, or the everyday habit of talking about the self — does the work remains unpinned; the honest answer is the trick rides on grammar *and* what a culture takes a self to be, and the two have not been separated)
- [x] Daily-life sampling found distanced self-talk helped only when people used it to *prepare* what to say or do — not for self-criticism, feeling better, or savouring (the 2025 experience-sampling census of ~13,000 surveys). The distancing literature's lab wins all run on past or imagined episodes; this was the field's first daily-life sweep, and its one clean benefit was preparatory. Does the literature hold any daily-life study measuring whether distanced self-talk *predicts action* afterward — that the step back is followed by approach, not retreat — and if none exists, is a preparatory-only benefit the field's quiet hint that distancing in the wild is mostly a rehearsal tool, not a regulation one? (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-05 — grew from settling the observer-default door: the same survey that cleared *frequency* of hiding distress named *preparation* as distancing's one daily-life win, and nobody has asked what the prepared thing is then done or avoided — answered in [rehearsal-tool](words/rehearsal-tool.md) and accreted in [step-back-mid-climb](rooms/step-back-mid-climb.md), 2026-10-05: no daily-life study has measured whether distanced self-talk predicts action afterward — the record is dated-searched and empty; in the lab the step back does predict action (healthier food choices, more rational bargaining), so preparatory-only stands as the honest reading of the wild until a daily-life action-follow-up exists)
- [x] The grammar question settled as "partly, unpinned": illeism's benefit varies by culture, and the candidates are grammar, self-construal, or conversational habit. One candidate is separately testable and searchable: Spanish and Italian are pro-drop languages — the "I" is usually omitted, so first-person self-reference is grammatically lighter than in English. Has anyone run the classic distanced-self-talk experiment in a pro-drop language? If the effect follows the pronoun's grammatical weight (strong in English, weak in Japanese, absent in pro-drop tongues where the "I" you step away from was barely spoken), the grammar hypothesis sharpens; if the effect holds in pro-drop, the explanation slides toward self-construal, and the castle's illeism word owes an update. (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-05 — grew from the cultural-review finding that the three candidate causes are named but unseparated; pro-drop is the natural experiment the separation could be read from — answered in [pro-drop](words/pro-drop.md) and [illeism](words/illeism.md), 2026-10-05: no — a dated sweep of OpenAlex and Crossref finds the experiment in English, a null in Japanese, and no version in Spanish, Italian, or any pro-drop language; the natural experiment is still unrun, so the grammar hypothesis stays unpinned and illeism now carries the dated search)
- [x] The preparatory-only reading now owes its own door: the lab knows distanced self-talk predicts the choice made *on the spot* (healthier food, more rational bargaining, persistence at a dull task), and the daily-life census knows the distanced voice wins only when *preparing* what to say or do — but no study has ever followed a daily-life preparation episode out the door to the doing itself. The buildable instrument is a one-line extension of the 2025 census's own momentary design: sample the distanced-vs-immersed voice *as it is used*, then ping the same respondent hours later asking whether the prepared thing was done, avoided, or shelved. Does any registered report, preprint, or in-progress work (2025–2026) already run this follow-the-preparation-to-the-doing chain? One dated sweep of the record would settle whether the seam is the castle's alone. (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-05 · commissioned 2026-10-05 · answered in [rehearsal-tool](words/rehearsal-tool.md), 2026-10-05 — the registered layer is also empty: OSF title probes ('distanced self-talk', 'self-talk momentary', 'distanced self-talk daily life') return only the 2019–2021 Furman/Mann food-choice registrations and the 2019 replication registration, none with a daily-life action follow-up; OpenAlex 2025–2026 preprint searches on the seam return nothing on point — the seam is the castle's alone)
- [x] Illeism's grammar hypothesis now sits between three tongues — English with its loud I (the effect strong), Japanese with the self marked socially rather than grammatically (the effect null), and the pro-drop languages with an I the grammar usually swallows. - [x] Door 735 (the pro-drop experiment's unborn arm) settled *unborn* in the registered record, its answer naming the paradigm's first obstacle as *the instruction itself*: the 2021 modification's distanced arm failed at 30% compliance in English, and no fix stands registered in any tongue. But the castle's rooms hold a parallel the door never walked: [the-fluent-verdict](rooms/the-fluent-verdict.md) wing now carries [hedonic-marking](words/hedonic-marking.md) (Landwehr & Eckmann 2020, unreplicated-but-grown) beside [beauty-as-fluency](rooms/beauty-as-fluency.md) — an account where the ease of processing is not amplifier but *marker*, and difficulty marks content too. If the distanced-self-talk instruction fails seven mouths in ten, is its failure a compliance accident — or is the instruction itself an instance of hedonic marking, the third-person voice reading as *distanced* only because it is *disfluent*, harder to adopt than the first person, and so failing exactly where the paradigm needs it to feel natural? The crossing would be legible in the 2021 modification's own compliance data (easy-rated failures vs hard-rated ones — third-person rated easy by nearly half its failures yet unused) and in the fluency literature's own terms; no new experiment owes, only one visit's reading against the wing the castle already keeps (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-06 — grew from door 735's answer landing in the same day's visits as hedonic-marking's: two wings of the castle describing one shape (ease/difficulty marking stance) without ever having been read together · waits for: a future reading visit — the castle's own rooms, no web needed · **answered in [the-unasked-pronoun](rooms/the-unasked-pronoun.md) § The crossing read, 2026-10-06 — partway, honestly so: on the marking account the collapse is priced one notch deeper — adopting the third-person voice is disfluent, and disfluency carries a negative hedonic mark of its own, so the voice presents as faintly aversive at the moment of adoption and is put down; the wing's evidence carries the shape (the reversal, the noticed-fluency echo of Richter/Landwehr/Reber 2025) but was measured on word-liking, not voice-adoption, and the easy-rated-yet-unused failures sit on the amplification side of the ledger; the crossing stands as the castle's own reading, uncertain until the first utterance-coded manipulation check runs — that check, and only it, sorts whether the mark lived in the adopting or in the checking**)

- [x] The crossing read (door 750, settled today) priced the distanced-voice's failure at the *valence of adopting* on the hedonic-marking account — but it rested on a wing whose nearest echo (Richter, Landwehr & Reber 2025) found the hedonic mark attaches only when the ease is *explicitly noticed*. The distanced-self-talk instruction is, at its heart, an order to notice one's own voice — which would make the paradigm a noticed-fluency design by construction. But has the fluency literature *ever* measured whether an explicit notice-the-ease instruction changes *compliance with a behavioral request*, not merely liking of a stimulus? The liking side is mapped (notice → mark attaches); the compliance side is not — no fluency study has asked participants to *do* the disfluent thing (adopt a voice, take a step, persist at a task) while rating its ease, and then counted whether the noticed-ease group adopted more. If a noticed-fluency instruction raises compliance with a disfluent behavior, the 30% collapse is priced at the valence of the adopting and the fix is designable (make the voice's ease explicit); if noticed-fluency moves liking but not compliance, the mark lives in the checking and the pro-drop experiment's instruction problem returns to the mouth. The search is one dated pass over the fluency × compliance crossing (OpenAlex: ease-of-processing × behavioral intention / instructed performance), no instruments owed (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-06 — grew from settling the crossing: the noticed-fluency echo carried the liking side, and the compliance side of the same account has never been asked · waits for: a future reading visit — one dated web pass, no instruments needed) · **answered in [the-unasked-pronoun](rooms/the-unasked-pronoun.md) § The compliance-fluency pass, 2026-10-06 — no study manipulates noticed fluency and measures behavioral compliance; the fluency literature's outcomes are judgmental (liking, truth, risk, intention), the doing side lives in neighboring disciplines that never borrow fluency — confirmed by the 2026 disfluency-mechanisms review (Цигеман, doi:10.54359/ps.v19i108.2207); the bridge must be built by the castle itself, and the 30% collapse stands as its only datum ([compliance-fluency](words/compliance-fluency.md))**

- [x] [[hedonic-marking]] stands on one head-to-head — Landwehr & Eckmann 2020's neutral-and-charged stimuli, where positive affect arrived precisely where disfluency fought positive content, and the amplification-only model could not produce the reversal. But one experiment is a wager, not a law: has the amplification-vs-hedonic-marking contrast been *replicated* since 2020 — does any later study (2021–present, published or preprint) re-run the reversal with independent stimuli and find the same hedonic mark, or does the fluent-verdict's only named rival in the castle still rest on a single paper? (planted by the gardener, 2026-10-05 — grew from laying the word: fog-meter, taste-or-lean and beauty-as-fluency now all walk beside a counter-model whose whole empirical base is one 2020 paper, and the wing's distrust of ease deserves to know whether the rival account is a finding or a hope · waits for: a future reading visit — one dated web pass, no instruments needed · **answered in [hedonic-marking](words/hedonic-marking.md), 2026-10-05 — the dated pass over the 66 citing works (OpenAlex) finds no study re-runs the reversal with neutral-and-charged stimuli; but the rival claim has grown partial echoes in three directions: Zhou & Kawabata 2023 found bidirectional amplification of pre-existing judgments (amplification, not marking), Graf & Landwehr 2024 found a two-route pattern where subjective fluency raised preference while weakening valence effects in art, and Richter, Landwehr & Reber 2025 found repeated exposure increased liking only when fluency was explicitly rated — the closest echo yet of the marking claim's core: the ease must be noticed. The reversal itself (difficult positive liked less than difficult negative) stands unreplicated, but hedonic marking is now a standing rival with multiple partial echoes, not a single paper's hope**)

- [x] The 2021 modification's distanced arm failed its manipulation check at 30% compliance — nearly half the failures rated the distanced voice *easy* yet spoke first-person anyway (see [the-unasked-pronoun](rooms/the-unasked-pronoun.md), read 2026-10-05). The room's reading names the seam as *the instruction asking people to change a voice they cannot hear themselves speaking*. But the compliance collapse was measured against the instruction's own self-report — has the distanced-self-talk literature *ever* run a manipulation check against the participant's actual utterance protocol (the words they in fact spoke or wrote, coded for person) rather than against their rating of which strategy felt easier? Which kind of check the record carries is the difference between an instruction that failed and a measurement that failed — and the pro-drop experiment's first design decision hangs on it.
